2a1(1): @##// O //rab 'byor la sogs pa dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po stong nyis brgya lnga bcu dang /byang chub sems dpa' chen po 'di lta ste

3a3(1): ldan nas/rang gi 'khor du ma der lhags so//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis sgra sgrogs bya ba'i/sems kyi rtog pa thugs su chud/sku'i gnas thams

3b2(1): thal mo sbyar nas//byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug la 'di skad ces smras so//kye ma mgon po thugs rje can//

3b7(1): du ma yis//rol mo sil snyan 'phan gdugs dang //bla bre rgyal mtshan lhab lhub dang //lda ldi na bza' du mas mchod //de nas mgon po spyan ras gzigs/pad+ma

7a1(1): @#//sems   de las byung ba'i 'phrin las kyis//nyon mongs las kyi sgrib pa sbyong byed//mgon po byams pas sngags bsgrub kyis//lam la ched du 'di

7a3(1): kyi snang ba'i phyir//'bad rtsol med ba'i sems kyis dkrol//rtsol ba med sems kyi dri mas ni//dri ma med pa'i sems 'ching 'gyur //nam mkha'i snying po'i sngags mi nga la//ched du 'di

9a1(1): @#//pos ni;byang chub rin chen gling 'jog mdzad////'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po pad+mo dra ba zhes bya ba las//
9a2(1): gleng gzhi'i le'u ste dang po'o//!//de nas bcom ldan thub pa che//mgon po yang dag rdzogs sangs rgyas//sna tshogs snang ba zhes bya ba'i//ting nge 'dzin la snyoms

20b4(1): ma la dz+nyA ni go ra/a san go bi ri ni pa sha ma mur rti/su kha ra pra te/sar+b+ba kle sha da sa na/bid+ha ma ni b+hi d+ha ma ni/bi na sha bi na sha/bi ni sha ya bi na sha ya/b+ha ga wa te/su ra sa bi ma la ba ti/sa ra sa ra/si ri si ri;
